  | open SinglePrimeFactor
  | 
  | theorem beq_true_eq {a b : Nat} (h : (a == b) = true) : a = b := by
  |   first
  |   | exact Nat.eq_of_beq_eq_true h
  |   | exact of_decide_eq_true h
  | 
  | theorem and_true_split {a b : Bool} (h : (a && b) = true) : a = true ∧ b = true := by
  |   cases a <;> cases b <;> first | exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩ | exact absurd h (by decide)
  | 
  | theorem loop_mem_to (x : Nat) : ∀ n (acc : List Nat), x ∈ List.range.loop n acc → x < n ∨ x ∈ acc := by
  |   intro n
  |   induction n with
  |   | zero => intro acc h; exact Or.inr h
  |   | succ n ih =>
  |     intro acc h
  |     rw [List.range.loop.eq_2] at h
  |     cases ih (n :: acc) h with
  |     | inl hl => exact Or.inl (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le hl (Nat.le_succ n))
  |     | inr hm =>
  |       cases hm with
  |       | head => exact Or.inl (Nat.lt_succ_self _)
  |       | tail _ h' => exact Or.inr h'
  | 
  | theorem loop_mem_of (x : Nat) : ∀ n (acc : List Nat), (x < n ∨ x ∈ acc) → x ∈ List.range.loop n acc := by
  |   intro n
  |   induction n with
  |   | zero =>
  |     intro acc h
  |     cases h with
  |     | inl hl => exact absurd hl (Nat.not_lt_zero x)
  |     | inr hm => exact hm
  |   | succ n ih =>
  |     intro acc h
  |     rw [List.range.loop.eq_2]
  |     apply ih
  |     cases h with
  |     | inl hl =>
  |       cases Nat.lt_or_ge x n with
  |       | inl hx => exact Or.inl hx
  |       | inr hx =>
  |         have e : x = n := Nat.le_antisymm (Nat.le_of_lt_succ hl) hx
  |         rw [e]; exact Or.inr (List.Mem.head _)
  |     | inr hm => exact Or.inr (List.Mem.tail _ hm)
  | 
  | theorem mem_range_of_lt {x n : Nat} (h : x < n) : x ∈ List.range n := by
  |   rw [List.range.eq_1]; exact loop_mem_of x n [] (Or.inl h)
  | 
  | theorem lt_of_mem_range {x n : Nat} (h : x ∈ List.range n) : x < n := by
  |   rw [List.range.eq_1] at h
  |   cases loop_mem_to x n [] h with
  |   | inl hl => exact hl
  |   | inr hm => cases hm
  | 
  | theorem mem_filter_of {α : Type} (p : α → Bool) (x : α) : ∀ l : List α, x ∈ l → p x = true → x ∈ l.filter p := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro hm; cases hm
  |   | cons a l ih =>
  |     intro hm hp
  |     cases hpa : p a with
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_t p a l hpa]
  |       cases hm with
  |       | head => exact List.Mem.head _
  |       | tail _ h' => exact List.Mem.tail _ (ih h' hp)
  |     | false =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_f p a l hpa]
  |       cases hm with
  |       | head => rw [hp] at hpa; exact absurd hpa (by decide)
  |       | tail _ h' => exact ih h' hp
  | 
  | theorem all_mem {α : Type} (f : α → Bool) (x : α) : ∀ l : List α, l.all f = true → x ∈ l → f x = true := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro _ hm; cases hm
  |   | cons a l ih =>
  |     intro h hm
  |     rw [List.all.eq_2] at h
  |     obtain ⟨ha, hl⟩ := and_true_split h
  |     cases hm with
  |     | head => exact ha
  |     | tail _ h' => exact ih hl h'
  | 
  | theorem isPrime_two_le {d : Nat} (h : isPrime d = true) : 2 ≤ d := by
  |   cases d with
  |   | zero => exact absurd h (by decide)
  |   | succ d =>
  |     cases d with
  |     | zero => exact absurd h (by decide)
  |     | succ d => exact Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.zero_le d))
  | 
  | theorem isPrime_nodiv {d : Nat} (h : isPrime d = true) (k : Nat) (hk : 2 ≤ k) (hkd : k < d) : d % k ≠ 0 := by
  |   unfold isPrime at h
  |   have hall : (List.range d).all (fun k => k == 0 || k == 1 || d % k != 0) = true := (and_true_split h).2
  |   have hk' := all_mem _ k _ hall (mem_range_of_lt hkd)
  |   intro hz
  |   cases k with
  |   | zero => exact absurd hk (by decide)
  |   | succ k =>
  |     cases k with
  |     | zero => exact absurd hk (by decide)
  |     | succ k =>
  |       have hz' : d % (k + 1 + 1) = 0 := hz
  |       rw [hz'] at hk'
  |       exact Bool.noConfusion hk'
  | 
  | theorem spf_prime (p : Nat) (h : singlePrimeFactor p = true) :
  |     ∃ r, isPrime r = true ∧ p % r = 0 ∧ ∀ d, isPrime d = true → p % d = 0 → d ≤ p → d = r := by
  |   have hl : (primeDivisors p).length = 1 := by unfold singlePrimeFactor at h; exact beq_true_eq h
  |   obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := length_one _ hl
  |   have hmem : r ∈ primeDivisors p := by rw [hr]; exact List.Mem.head _
  |   have hpr := mem_filter_pred _ r _ hmem
  |   obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := and_true_split hpr
  |   refine ⟨r, h1, beq_true_eq h2, ?_⟩
  |   intro d hd hpd hdp
  |   have hin : d ∈ primeDivisors p := by
  |     unfold primeDivisors
  |     apply mem_filter_of _ d _ (mem_range_of_lt (Nat.lt_succ_of_le hdp))
  |     show (isPrime d && p % d == 0) = true
  |     rw [hd, hpd]
  |     decide
  |   rw [hr] at hin
  |   cases hin with
  |   | head => rfl
  |   | tail _ h' => cases h'
  | 
  | 
  | theorem all_false_exists {α : Type} (f : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, l.all f = false → ∃ x, x ∈ l ∧ f x = false := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro h; exact Bool.noConfusion h
  |   | cons a l ih =>
  |     intro h
  |     rw [List.all.eq_2] at h
  |     cases hfa : f a with
  |     | false => exact ⟨a, List.Mem.head _, hfa⟩
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [hfa] at h
  |       obtain ⟨x, hx, hfx⟩ := ih h
  |       exact ⟨x, List.Mem.tail _ hx, hfx⟩
  | 
  | theorem mod_self' (m : Nat) (hm : 0 < m) : m % m = 0 := by
  |   have e := mul_mod_self' m 1 hm
  |   rw [Nat.mul_one] at e
  |   exact e
  | 
  | theorem mod_trans {a b d : Nat} (hb : 0 < b) (hd : 0 < d) (hab : a % b = 0) (hbd : b % d = 0) : a % d = 0 := by
  |   obtain ⟨c1, h1⟩ := mod_zero_witness a b hb hab
  |   obtain ⟨c2, h2⟩ := mod_zero_witness b d hd hbd
  |   rw [h1, h2, ← B329.mul_assoc']
  |   exact mul_mod_self' d _ hd
  | 
  | theorem le_of_mod_zero {a d : Nat} (ha : 0 < a) (hd : 0 < d) (h : a % d = 0) : d ≤ a := by
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness a d hd h
  |   cases c with
  |   | zero => rw [Nat.mul_zero] at hc; rw [hc] at ha; exact absurd ha (Nat.lt_irrefl 0)
  |   | succ c => rw [hc, Nat.mul_succ]; exact Nat.le_add_left d (d * c)
  | 
  | theorem exists_prime_div : ∀ m, 2 ≤ m → ∃ d, isPrime d = true ∧ m % d = 0 ∧ d ≤ m := by
  |   intro m
  |   induction m using Nat.strongRecOn with
  |   | _ m ih =>
  |     intro hm
  |     have hm0 : 0 < m := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hm
  |     cases hp : isPrime m with
  |     | true => exact ⟨m, hp, mod_self' m hm0, Nat.le_refl m⟩
  |     | false =>
  |       cases m with
  |       | zero => exact absurd hm (by decide)
  |       | succ m1 =>
  |         cases m1 with
  |         | zero => exact absurd hm (by decide)
  |         | succ k =>
  |           have hall : (List.range (k + 1 + 1)).all
  |               (fun j => j == 0 || j == 1 || (k + 1 + 1) % j != 0) = false := hp
  |           obtain ⟨j, hj, hfj⟩ := all_false_exists _ _ hall
  |           have hjm : j < k + 1 + 1 := lt_of_mem_range hj
  |           cases j with
  |           | zero => exact Bool.noConfusion hfj
  |           | succ j1 =>
  |             cases j1 with
  |             | zero => exact Bool.noConfusion hfj
  |             | succ i =>
  |               have hdiv : (k + 1 + 1) % (i + 1 + 1) = 0 := by
  |                 cases hb : ((k + 1 + 1) % (i + 1 + 1) == 0) with
  |                 | true => exact beq_true_eq hb
  |                 | false =>
  |                   have hf2 : ((k + 1 + 1) % (i + 1 + 1) != 0) = false := hfj
  |                   rw [bne, hb] at hf2
  |                   exact Bool.noConfusion hf2
  |               have hi2 : 2 ≤ i + 1 + 1 := Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.zero_le i))
  |               obtain ⟨d, hd, hid, hdi⟩ := ih (i + 1 + 1) hjm hi2
  |               have hd0 : 0 < d := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) (isPrime_two_le hd)
  |               exact ⟨d, hd, mod_trans (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hi2) hd0 hdiv hid,
  |                      Nat.le_trans hdi (Nat.le_of_lt hjm)⟩
  | 
  | theorem spf_power (p : Nat) (h : singlePrimeFactor p = true) :
  |     ∃ r e, isPrime r = true ∧ 1 ≤ e ∧ p = r ^ e := by
  |   obtain ⟨r, hr, hpr, huniq⟩ := spf_prime p h
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   cases p with
  |   | zero => exact absurd h (by decide)
  |   | succ p' =>
  |     obtain ⟨j, u, hju, hu⟩ := B329.valuation_exists r hr2 (p' + 1) (Nat.succ_pos p')
  |     have hu0 : 0 < u := by
  |       cases u with
  |       | zero => rw [Nat.zero_mul] at hju; exact Nat.noConfusion hju
  |       | succ u' => exact Nat.succ_pos u'
  |     have hu1 : u = 1 := by
  |       cases Nat.lt_or_ge u 2 with
  |       | inl hlt => exact Nat.le_antisymm (Nat.le_of_lt_succ hlt) hu0
  |       | inr hge =>
  |         exfalso
  |         obtain ⟨d, hd, hud, _⟩ := exists_prime_div u hge
  |         have hd0 : 0 < d := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) (isPrime_two_le hd)
  |         obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness u d hd0 hud
  |         have hpd : (p' + 1) % d = 0 := by
  |           rw [hju, hc, ← B329.mul_assoc']
  |           exact mul_mod_self' d _ hd0
  |         have hdp : d ≤ p' + 1 := le_of_mod_zero (Nat.succ_pos p') hd0 hpd
  |         have e := huniq d hd hpd hdp
  |         rw [e] at hc
  |         exact hu c hc
  |     refine ⟨r, j, hr, ?_, ?_⟩
  |     · cases j with
  |       | zero =>
  |         exfalso
  |         rw [Nat.pow_zero, Nat.mul_one, hu1] at hju
  |         rw [hju] at hpr
  |         have e1 : 1 % r = 1 := mod_unique 1 r 0 1 hr0 hr2 (by rw [Nat.mul_zero, Nat.zero_add])
  |         rw [e1] at hpr
  |         exact Nat.noConfusion hpr
  |       | succ j' => exact Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.zero_le j')
  |     · rw [hju, hu1, Nat.one_mul]
  | 
  | 
  | theorem mul_pos' {a b : Nat} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) : 0 < a * b := by
  |   cases a with
  |   | zero => exact absurd ha (Nat.lt_irrefl 0)
  |   | succ a =>
  |     cases b with
  |     | zero => exact absurd hb (Nat.lt_irrefl 0)
  |     | succ b => rw [Nat.mul_succ]; exact Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.succ_pos a) (Nat.le_add_left _ _)
  | 
  | theorem pow_pos' (r : Nat) (hr : 0 < r) : ∀ j, 0 < r ^ j := by
  |   intro j
  |   induction j with
  |   | zero => rw [Nat.pow_zero]; exact Nat.succ_pos 0
  |   | succ j ih => rw [Nat.pow_succ]; exact mul_pos' ih hr
  | 
  | theorem euclid_lt (r b : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) : ∀ a, 0 < a → a < r → (a * b) % r = 0 → b % r = 0 := by
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   intro a
  |   induction a using Nat.strongRecOn with
  |   | _ a ih =>
  |     intro ha0 har hab
  |     cases Nat.lt_or_ge a 2 with
  |     | inl h1 =>
  |       have e : a = 1 := Nat.le_antisymm (Nat.le_of_lt_succ h1) ha0
  |       rw [e, Nat.one_mul] at hab
  |       exact hab
  |     | inr h2 =>
  |       have ha0' : 0 < a := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) h2
  |       obtain ⟨q, hq, ht⟩ := mod_spec r a ha0'
  |       have hnz : r % a ≠ 0 := isPrime_nodiv hr a h2 har
  |       generalize hg : r % a = t at hq ht hnz
  |       have ht0 : 0 < t := by
  |         cases t with
  |         | zero => exact absurd rfl hnz
  |         | succ z => exact Nat.succ_pos z
  |       apply ih t ht ht0 (Nat.lt_trans ht har)
  |       obtain ⟨c1, hc1⟩ := mod_zero_witness (a * b) r hr0 hab
  |       have e : t * b + r * (c1 * q) = r * b := by
  |         calc t * b + r * (c1 * q) = t * b + r * c1 * q := by rw [B329.mul_assoc']
  |           _ = t * b + a * b * q := by rw [← hc1]
  |           _ = t * b + a * q * b := by rw [← B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm b q, B329.mul_assoc']
  |           _ = (a * q + t) * b := by
  |               rw [Nat.mul_comm (a * q + t) b, Nat.mul_add, Nat.mul_comm b (a * q), Nat.mul_comm b t, Nat.add_comm]
  |           _ = r * b := by rw [← hq]
  |       obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := B329.multiple_of_add_multiple (c1 * q) b e
  |       rw [hd]
  |       exact mul_mod_self' r d hr0
  | 
  | theorem euclid (r a b : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (hab : (a * b) % r = 0) (ha : a % r ≠ 0) : b % r = 0 := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) (isPrime_two_le hr)
  |   obtain ⟨q, hq, hlt⟩ := mod_spec a r hr0
  |   generalize hg : a % r = a' at hq hlt ha
  |   have ha0 : 0 < a' := by
  |     cases a' with
  |     | zero => exact absurd rfl ha
  |     | succ z => exact Nat.succ_pos z
  |   apply euclid_lt r b hr a' ha0 hlt
  |   obtain ⟨c1, hc1⟩ := mod_zero_witness (a * b) r hr0 hab
  |   have e : a' * b + r * (q * b) = r * c1 := by
  |     calc a' * b + r * (q * b) = a' * b + r * q * b := by rw [B329.mul_assoc']
  |       _ = (r * q + a') * b := by
  |           rw [Nat.mul_comm (r * q + a') b, Nat.mul_add, Nat.mul_comm b (r * q), Nat.mul_comm b a', Nat.add_comm]
  |       _ = a * b := by rw [← hq]
  |       _ = r * c1 := hc1
  |   obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := B329.multiple_of_add_multiple (q * b) c1 e
  |   rw [hd]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' r d hr0
  | 
  | theorem euclid_pow (r s : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (hs : s % r ≠ 0) :
  |     ∀ j x, (x * s) % (r ^ j) = 0 → x % (r ^ j) = 0 := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) (isPrime_two_le hr)
  |   intro j
  |   induction j with
  |   | zero =>
  |     intro x _
  |     rw [Nat.pow_zero]
  |     exact mod_unique x 1 x 0 (by decide) (by decide) (by rw [Nat.one_mul, Nat.add_zero])
  |   | succ j ih =>
  |     intro x h
  |     have hrj0 : 0 < r ^ (j + 1) := pow_pos' r hr0 (j + 1)
  |     obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness (x * s) (r ^ (j + 1)) hrj0 h
  |     have hr_xs : (s * x) % r = 0 := by
  |       rw [Nat.mul_comm s x, hc, Nat.pow_succ, Nat.mul_comm (r ^ j) r, ← B329.mul_assoc']
  |       exact mul_mod_self' r _ hr0
  |     have hrx : x % r = 0 := euclid r s x hr hr_xs hs
  |     obtain ⟨x1, hx1⟩ := mod_zero_witness x r hr0 hrx
  |     have e : r * (x1 * s) = r * (r ^ j * c) := by
  |       rw [B329.mul_assoc', ← hx1, hc, Nat.pow_succ, Nat.mul_comm (r ^ j) r, ← B329.mul_assoc']
  |     have e2 : x1 * s = r ^ j * c := Nat.eq_of_mul_eq_mul_left hr0 e
  |     have h1 : (x1 * s) % (r ^ j) = 0 := by rw [e2]; exact mul_mod_self' _ _ (pow_pos' r hr0 j)
  |     obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := mod_zero_witness x1 (r ^ j) (pow_pos' r hr0 j) (ih x1 h1)
  |     rw [hx1, hd, B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm r (r ^ j), ← Nat.pow_succ]
  |     exact mul_mod_self' _ _ hrj0
  | 
  | 
  | def ind (b : Bool) : Nat := match b with | true => 1 | false => 0
  | def sumf {α : Type} (f : α → Nat) (l : List α) : Nat := l.foldl (fun acc x => acc + f x) 0
  | 
  | theorem foldl_shift {α : Type} (f : α → Nat) : ∀ (l : List α) (a : Nat),
  |     l.foldl (fun acc x => acc + f x) a = a + l.foldl (fun acc x => acc + f x) 0 := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro a; rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     intro a
  |     rw [List.foldl_cons, List.foldl_cons, ih (a + f x), ih (0 + f x), Nat.zero_add, Nat.add_assoc]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_nil {α : Type} (f : α → Nat) : sumf f [] = 0 := rfl
  | 
  | theorem sumf_cons {α : Type} (f : α → Nat) (x : α) (l : List α) : sumf f (x :: l) = f x + sumf f l := by
  |   unfold sumf
  |   rw [List.foldl_cons, foldl_shift f l (0 + f x), Nat.zero_add]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_congr {α : Type} (f g : α → Nat) : ∀ l : List α, (∀ x, x ∈ l → f x = g x) → sumf f l = sumf g l := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro _; rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     intro h
  |     rw [sumf_cons, sumf_cons, h x (List.Mem.head _), ih (fun y hy => h y (List.Mem.tail _ hy))]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_zero {α : Type} : ∀ l : List α, sumf (fun _ => 0) l = 0 := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l ih => rw [sumf_cons, ih]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_add {α : Type} (f g : α → Nat) : ∀ l : List α, sumf (fun x => f x + g x) l = sumf f l + sumf g l := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     rw [sumf_cons, sumf_cons, sumf_cons, ih, Nat.add_add_add_comm]
  | 
  | theorem length_filter_ind {α : Type} (P : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, (l.filter P).length = sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     rw [sumf_cons]
  |     cases hp : P x with
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_t P x l hp, List.length_cons, ih]
  |       show sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l + 1 = 1 + sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l
  |       rw [Nat.add_comm]
  |     | false =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_f P x l hp, ih]
  |       show sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l = 0 + sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l
  |       rw [Nat.zero_add]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_swap {α β : Type} (g : α → β → Nat) (S : List β) : ∀ U : List α,
  |     sumf (fun u => sumf (fun s => g u s) S) U = sumf (fun s => sumf (fun u => g u s) U) S := by
  |   intro U
  |   induction U with
  |   | nil => rw [sumf_nil]; exact (sumf_zero S).symm
  |   | cons u U ih =>
  |     rw [sumf_cons, ih,
  |         sumf_congr (fun s => sumf (fun u' => g u' s) (u :: U)) (fun s => g u s + sumf (fun u' => g u' s) U) S
  |           (fun s _ => sumf_cons _ u U),
  |         sumf_add]
  | 
  | theorem count_swap {α β : Type} (P : α → β → Bool) (U : List α) (S : List β) :
  |     sumf (fun u => (S.filter (P u)).length) U = sumf (fun s => (U.filter (fun u => P u s)).length) S := by
  |   rw [sumf_congr _ (fun u => sumf (fun s => ind (P u s)) S) U (fun u _ => length_filter_ind (P u) S),
  |       sumf_congr _ (fun s => sumf (fun u => ind (P u s)) U) S (fun s _ => length_filter_ind (fun u => P u s) U)]
  |   exact sumf_swap (fun u s => ind (P u s)) S U
  | 
  | theorem sumA_swap (p n m : Nat) :
  |     (B329.units p n).foldl (fun acc u => acc + B329.offBallFixed p n u m) 0 =
  |     sumf (fun s => ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)).length)
  |       (List.range (B329.gridN p n)) :=
  |   count_swap (fun u s => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)
  |     (B329.units p n) (List.range (B329.gridN p n))
  | 
  | theorem sumAN_swap (p n : Nat) : B329.sumAN p n =
  |     sumf (fun s => ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.gridN p n == 0)).length)
  |       (List.range (B329.gridN p n)) := sumA_swap p n (B329.gridN p n)
  | 
  | theorem sumAQ_swap (p n : Nat) : B329.sumAQ p n =
  |     sumf (fun s => ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.ballQ p n == 0)).length)
  |       (List.range (B329.gridN p n)) := sumA_swap p n (B329.ballQ p n)
  | 
  | 
  | theorem append_assoc' {α : Type} : ∀ (a b c : List α), (a ++ b) ++ c = a ++ (b ++ c) := by
  |   intro a b c
  |   induction a with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x a ih => show x :: ((a ++ b) ++ c) = x :: (a ++ (b ++ c)); rw [ih]
  | 
  | theorem length_append' {α : Type} : ∀ (l1 l2 : List α), (l1 ++ l2).length = l1.length + l2.length := by
  |   intro l1 l2
  |   induction l1 with
  |   | nil => show l2.length = 0 + l2.length; rw [Nat.zero_add]
  |   | cons x l1 ih =>
  |     show (x :: (l1 ++ l2)).length = (x :: l1).length + l2.length
  |     rw [List.length_cons, List.length_cons, ih, Nat.add_right_comm]
  | 
  | theorem filter_append' {α : Type} (P : α → Bool) : ∀ (l1 l2 : List α), (l1 ++ l2).filter P = l1.filter P ++ l2.filter P := by
  |   intro l1 l2
  |   induction l1 with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l1 ih =>
  |     show (x :: (l1 ++ l2)).filter P = (x :: l1).filter P ++ l2.filter P
  |     cases hp : P x with
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_t P x _ hp, filter_cons_t P x _ hp, ih]
  |       all_goals rfl
  |     | false => rw [filter_cons_f P x _ hp, filter_cons_f P x _ hp, ih]
  | 
  | theorem loop_append : ∀ (n : Nat) (acc : List Nat), List.range.loop n acc = List.range.loop n [] ++ acc := by
  |   intro n
  |   induction n with
  |   | zero => intro acc; rfl
  |   | succ n ih =>
  |     intro acc
  |     rw [List.range.loop.eq_2, List.range.loop.eq_2, ih (n :: acc), ih [n], append_assoc']
  |     all_goals rfl
  | 
  | theorem range_succ' (n : Nat) : List.range (n + 1) = List.range n ++ [n] := by
  |   rw [List.range.eq_1, List.range.eq_1, List.range.loop.eq_2, loop_append n [n]]
  | 
  | def cnt (d t N : Nat) : Nat := ((List.range N).filter (fun u => u % d == t)).length
  | 
  | theorem cnt_succ (d t N : Nat) : cnt d t (N + 1) = cnt d t N + ind (N % d == t) := by
  |   unfold cnt
  |   rw [range_succ', filter_append', length_append']
  |   cases h : (N % d == t) with
  |   | true => rw [filter_cons_t _ N [] h]; all_goals rfl
  |   | false => rw [filter_cons_f _ N [] h]; all_goals rfl
  | 
  | theorem ne_beq_false {a b : Nat} (h : a ≠ b) : (a == b) = false := by
  |   cases hb : (a == b) with
  |   | true => exact absurd (beq_true_eq hb) h
  |   | false => rfl
  | 
  | theorem ind_step (t i : Nat) : ind (decide (t < i)) + ind (i == t) = ind (decide (t < i + 1)) := by
  |   cases Nat.lt_or_ge t i with
  |   | inl h1 =>
  |     have hne : i ≠ t := fun e => Nat.lt_irrefl t (by rw [e] at h1; exact h1)
  |     rw [decide_eq_true h1, ne_beq_false hne, decide_eq_true (Nat.lt_succ_of_le (Nat.le_of_lt h1))]
  |     all_goals rfl
  |   | inr h2 =>
  |     cases Nat.lt_or_ge i t with
  |     | inl h3 =>
  |       have hne : i ≠ t := fun e => Nat.lt_irrefl t (by rw [e] at h3; exact h3)
  |       have hn1 : ¬ t < i + 1 := fun h => Nat.lt_irrefl t (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le h (Nat.succ_le_of_lt h3))
  |       have hn0 : ¬ t < i := fun h => Nat.lt_irrefl t (Nat.lt_trans h h3)
  |       rw [decide_eq_false hn0, ne_beq_false hne, decide_eq_false hn1]
  |       all_goals rfl
  |     | inr h4 =>
  |       have e : i = t := Nat.le_antisymm h2 h4
  |       have hn0 : ¬ t < i := fun h => Nat.lt_irrefl t (by rw [e] at h; exact h)
  |       rw [decide_eq_false hn0, e, decide_eq_true (Nat.lt_succ_self t)]
  |       have hb : (t == t) = true := by first | exact Nat.beq_refl t | exact decide_eq_true rfl
  |       rw [hb]
  |       all_goals rfl
  | 
  | theorem blk (d t c : Nat) (hd : 0 < d) (h : cnt d t (d * c) = c) :
  |     ∀ i, i ≤ d → cnt d t (d * c + i) = c + ind (decide (t < i)) := by
  |   intro i
  |   induction i with
  |   | zero => intro _; rw [Nat.add_zero, h]; all_goals rfl
  |   | succ i ih =>
  |     intro hi
  |     have hi' : i < d := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.lt_succ_self i) hi
  |     rw [← Nat.add_assoc, cnt_succ, ih (Nat.le_of_lt hi'),
  |         mod_unique (d * c + i) d c i hd hi' rfl, Nat.add_assoc, ind_step]
  | 
  | theorem ap_count (d t : Nat) (hd : 0 < d) (ht : t < d) : ∀ c, cnt d t (d * c) = c := by
  |   intro c
  |   induction c with
  |   | zero => rw [Nat.mul_zero]; rfl
  |   | succ c ih =>
  |     have e := blk d t c hd ih d (Nat.le_refl d)
  |     rw [decide_eq_true ht] at e
  |     rw [Nat.mul_succ, e]
  |     all_goals rfl
  | 
  | 
  | theorem filter_congr' {α : Type} (P Q : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, (∀ x, x ∈ l → P x = Q x) → l.filter P = l.filter Q := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro _; rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     intro h
  |     have hx := h x (List.Mem.head _)
  |     have ht := ih (fun y hy => h y (List.Mem.tail _ hy))
  |     cases hp : P x with
  |     | true => rw [filter_cons_t P x l hp, filter_cons_t Q x l (by rw [← hx, hp]), ht]
  |     | false => rw [filter_cons_f P x l hp, filter_cons_f Q x l (by rw [← hx, hp]), ht]
  | 
  | theorem filter_filter' {α : Type} (P Q : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, (l.filter P).filter Q = l.filter (fun x => P x && Q x) := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     cases hp : P x with
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_t P x l hp]
  |       cases hq : Q x with
  |       | true =>
  |         rw [filter_cons_t Q x _ hq,
  |             filter_cons_t (fun x => P x && Q x) x l (by show (P x && Q x) = true; rw [hp, hq]; all_goals rfl), ih]
  |       | false =>
  |         rw [filter_cons_f Q x _ hq,
  |             filter_cons_f (fun x => P x && Q x) x l (by show (P x && Q x) = false; rw [hp, hq]; all_goals rfl), ih]
  |     | false =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_f P x l hp,
  |           filter_cons_f (fun x => P x && Q x) x l (by show (P x && Q x) = false; rw [hp]; all_goals rfl), ih]
  | 
  | theorem pow_mul' (r e : Nat) : ∀ n, (r ^ e) ^ n = r ^ (e * n) := by
  |   intro n
  |   induction n with
  |   | zero => rw [Nat.pow_zero, Nat.mul_zero, Nat.pow_zero]
  |   | succ n ih => rw [Nat.pow_succ, ih, Nat.mul_succ, B329.pow_add']
  | 
  | theorem mod_mod_of_mul (a q k : Nat) (hq : 0 < q) (hk : 0 < k) : (a % (q * k)) % q = a % q := by
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc, _⟩ := mod_spec a (q * k) (mul_pos' hq hk)
  |   have e : a = a % (q * k) + q * (k * c) := by rw [B329.mul_assoc', Nat.add_comm]; exact hc
  |   calc (a % (q * k)) % q = (a % (q * k) + q * (k * c)) % q := (add_mul_mod' _ q _ hq).symm
  |     _ = a % q := by rw [← e]
  | 
  | theorem pow_cancel (r x k L : Nat) (hr : 0 < r) (hkL : k ≤ L) (h : (x * r ^ k) % (r ^ L) = 0) :
  |     x % (r ^ (L - k)) = 0 := by
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness _ _ (pow_pos' r hr L) h
  |   have e : r ^ L = r ^ (L - k) * r ^ k := by rw [← B329.pow_add', sub_add_cancel' hkL]
  |   have e2 : x * r ^ k = r ^ (L - k) * c * r ^ k := by
  |     rw [hc, e, ← B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm (r ^ k) c, B329.mul_assoc']
  |   have e3 : x = r ^ (L - k) * c := Nat.eq_of_mul_eq_mul_right (pow_pos' r hr k) e2
  |   rw [e3]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' _ _ (pow_pos' r hr _)
  | 
  | theorem offball_val (r e n s : Nat) (hr2 : 2 ≤ r) (hs : s % (r ^ (e * n)) ≠ 0) :
  |     ∃ k s', s = s' * r ^ k ∧ B329.NotDiv r s' ∧ k < e * n := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   have hs0 : 0 < s := by
  |     cases s with
  |     | zero => exact absurd (Nat.zero_mod _) hs
  |     | succ s => exact Nat.succ_pos s
  |   obtain ⟨j, u, hju, hu⟩ := B329.valuation_exists r hr2 s hs0
  |   refine ⟨j, u, hju, hu, ?_⟩
  |   apply Nat.lt_of_not_le
  |   intro hle
  |   apply hs
  |   have e1 : r ^ j = r ^ (j - e * n) * r ^ (e * n) := by rw [← B329.pow_add', sub_add_cancel' hle]
  |   rw [hju, e1, B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm _ (r ^ (e * n))]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' _ _ (pow_pos' r hr0 _)
  | 
  | theorem bne_true_ne {a b : Nat} (h : (a != b) = true) : a ≠ b := by
  |   intro e
  |   rw [e] at h
  |   have hb : (b == b) = true := by first | exact Nat.beq_refl b | exact decide_eq_true rfl
  |   rw [bne, hb] at h
  |   exact Bool.noConfusion h
  | 
  | theorem ne_bne_true {a b : Nat} (h : a ≠ b) : (a != b) = true := by
  |   rw [bne, ne_beq_false h]; all_goals rfl
  | 
  | theorem notDiv_of_mod {r u : Nat} (hr : 0 < r) (h : u % r ≠ 0) : B329.NotDiv r u := by
  |   intro c hc
  |   apply h
  |   rw [hc]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' r c hr
  | 
  | theorem mod_of_notDiv {r u : Nat} (hr : 0 < r) (h : B329.NotDiv r u) : u % r ≠ 0 := by
  |   intro hz
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness u r hr hz
  |   exact h c hc
  | 
  | 
  | theorem one_le_sub {k L : Nat} (h : k < L) : 1 ≤ L - k := by
  |   cases hz : L - k with
  |   | zero =>
  |     exfalso
  |     have e := sub_add_cancel' (Nat.le_of_lt h)
  |     rw [hz, Nat.zero_add] at e
  |     rw [e] at h
  |     exact Nat.lt_irrefl k h
  |   | succ z => exact Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.zero_le z)
  | 
  | theorem pow_ge (r j : Nat) (hr : 0 < r) (hj : 1 ≤ j) : r ≤ r ^ j := by
  |   have e : r ^ j = r ^ (j - 1) * r := by rw [← Nat.pow_succ, sub_add_cancel' hj]
  |   rw [e]
  |   have h1 : 1 ≤ r ^ (j - 1) := pow_pos' r hr (j - 1)
  |   calc r = 1 * r := (Nat.one_mul r).symm
  |     _ ≤ r ^ (j - 1) * r := Nat.mul_le_mul_right r h1
  | 
  | theorem pow_mod_self (r j : Nat) (hr : 0 < r) (hj : 1 ≤ j) : (r ^ j) % r = 0 := by
  |   have e : r ^ j = r * r ^ (j - 1) := by rw [Nat.mul_comm, ← Nat.pow_succ, sub_add_cancel' hj]
  |   rw [e]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' r _ hr
  | 
  | theorem mod_r_one (r u j : Nat) (hr2 : 2 ≤ r) (hj : 1 ≤ j) (h : u % r ^ j = 1) : u % r = 1 := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc, _⟩ := mod_spec u (r ^ j) (pow_pos' r hr0 j)
  |   rw [h] at hc
  |   have e : r ^ j = r * r ^ (j - 1) := by rw [Nat.mul_comm, ← Nat.pow_succ, sub_add_cancel' hj]
  |   rw [e, ← B329.mul_assoc'] at hc
  |   exact mod_unique u r (r ^ (j - 1) * c) 1 hr0 (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2) hc
  | 
  | theorem mod_one_not_dvd (r u j : Nat) (hr2 : 2 ≤ r) (hj : 1 ≤ j) (h : u % r = 1) : u % r ^ j ≠ 0 := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   intro hz
  |   have e := mod_trans (pow_pos' r hr0 j) hr0 hz (pow_mod_self r j hr0 hj)
  |   rw [h] at e
  |   exact Nat.noConfusion e
  | 
  | theorem cong_of (r s' k L u : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (hs' : s' % r ≠ 0) (hkL : k < L) (hu : 0 < u)
  |     (h : ((u - 1) * (s' * r ^ k)) % (r ^ L) = 0) : u % (r ^ (L - k)) = 1 := by
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   rw [B329.mul_assoc'] at h
  |   have h2 := pow_cancel r ((u - 1) * s') k L hr0 (Nat.le_of_lt hkL) h
  |   have h3 := euclid_pow r s' hr hs' (L - k) (u - 1) h2
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness (u - 1) (r ^ (L - k)) (pow_pos' r hr0 _) h3
  |   have hu1 : 1 ≤ u := hu
  |   have e : u = r ^ (L - k) * c + 1 := by rw [← hc, sub_add_cancel' hu1]
  |   exact mod_unique u (r ^ (L - k)) c 1 (pow_pos' r hr0 _)
  |     (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2) (pow_ge r (L - k) hr0 (one_le_sub hkL))) e
  | 
  | theorem cong_to (r s' k L u : Nat) (hr0 : 0 < r) (hkL : k ≤ L) (h : u % (r ^ (L - k)) = 1) :
  |     ((u - 1) * (s' * r ^ k)) % (r ^ L) = 0 := by
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc, _⟩ := mod_spec u (r ^ (L - k)) (pow_pos' r hr0 _)
  |   rw [h] at hc
  |   have e1 : u - 1 = r ^ (L - k) * c := by rw [hc]; all_goals rfl
  |   have e2 : (u - 1) * (s' * r ^ k) = r ^ L * (c * s') := by
  |     rw [e1]
  |     calc r ^ (L - k) * c * (s' * r ^ k) = r ^ (L - k) * (c * (s' * r ^ k)) :=
  |           (B329.mul_assoc' (r ^ (L - k)) c (s' * r ^ k)).symm
  |       _ = r ^ (L - k) * (c * s' * r ^ k) := congrArg (fun x => r ^ (L - k) * x) (B329.mul_assoc' c s' (r ^ k))
  |       _ = r ^ (L - k) * (r ^ k * (c * s')) := congrArg (fun x => r ^ (L - k) * x) (Nat.mul_comm (c * s') (r ^ k))
  |       _ = r ^ (L - k) * r ^ k * (c * s') := B329.mul_assoc' (r ^ (L - k)) (r ^ k) (c * s')
  |       _ = r ^ L * (c * s') := by rw [← B329.pow_add', sub_add_cancel' hkL]
  |   rw [e2]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' _ _ (pow_pos' r hr0 L)
  | 
  | theorem offball_of (r s' k E u : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (hs' : s' % r ≠ 0) (hk : k < E) (hu : u % r = 1) :
  |     (u * (s' * r ^ k)) % (r ^ E) ≠ 0 := by
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   intro hz
  |   rw [B329.mul_assoc'] at hz
  |   have h2 := pow_cancel r (u * s') k E hr0 (Nat.le_of_lt hk) hz
  |   have h3 := euclid_pow r s' hr hs' (E - k) u h2
  |   exact mod_one_not_dvd r u (E - k) hr2 (one_le_sub hk) hu h3
  | 
  | theorem point (r e E L k s' p q N m s u : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (he : 1 ≤ e) (hs' : s' % r ≠ 0)
  |     (hk : k < E) (hEL : E ≤ L) (hp : p = r ^ e) (hq : q = r ^ E) (hN : N = q * q) (hm : m = r ^ L)
  |     (hs : s = s' * r ^ k) (hoff : s % q ≠ 0) :
  |     ((u % p != 0) && (!(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0))
  |       = (u % r ^ (L - k) == 1) := by
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   have hkL : k < L := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le hk hEL
  |   cases hb : (u % r ^ (L - k) == 1) with
  |   | true =>
  |     have h1 : u % r ^ (L - k) = 1 := beq_true_eq hb
  |     have hur : u % r = 1 := mod_r_one r u (L - k) hr2 (one_le_sub hkL) h1
  |     have c1 : (u % p != 0) = true := ne_bne_true (by rw [hp]; exact mod_one_not_dvd r u e hr2 he hur)
  |     have c2 : (!(s % q == 0)) = true := by rw [ne_beq_false hoff]; all_goals rfl
  |     have hq0 : 0 < q := by rw [hq]; exact pow_pos' r hr0 E
  |     have hus : (u * s) % q ≠ 0 := by rw [hs, hq]; exact offball_of r s' k E u hr hs' hk hur
  |     have c3 : (!((u * s) % N % q == 0)) = true := by
  |       rw [hN, mod_mod_of_mul (u * s) q q hq0 hq0, ne_beq_false hus]; all_goals rfl
  |     have c4 : (((u - 1) * s) % m == 0) = true := by
  |       rw [hs, hm, cong_to r s' k L u hr0 (Nat.le_of_lt hkL) h1]; all_goals rfl
  |     rw [c1, c2, c3, c4]
  |     all_goals rfl
  |   | false =>
  |     cases hL : ((u % p != 0) && (!(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)) with
  |     | false => rfl
  |     | true =>
  |       exfalso
  |       obtain ⟨a1, a234⟩ := and_true_split hL
  |       obtain ⟨_, a4⟩ := and_true_split a234
  |       have hu0 : 0 < u := by
  |         cases u with
  |         | zero => exact absurd (Nat.zero_mod p) (bne_true_ne a1)
  |         | succ u' => exact Nat.succ_pos u'
  |       have h4 : ((u - 1) * s) % m = 0 := beq_true_eq a4
  |       rw [hs, hm] at h4
  |       have h5 := cong_of r s' k L u hr hs' hkL hu0 h4
  |       rw [h5] at hb
  |       exact Bool.noConfusion hb
  | 
  | theorem count_is (D c N : Nat) (hD : 1 < D) (h : N = D * c) : cnt D 1 N = c := by
  |   rw [h]
  |   exact ap_count D 1 (Nat.lt_trans (by decide) hD) hD c
  | 
  | theorem per_s_count (r e E L k s' p q N m s : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (he : 1 ≤ e) (hs' : s' % r ≠ 0)
  |     (hk : k < E) (hEL : E ≤ L) (hp : p = r ^ e) (hq : q = r ^ E) (hN : N = q * q) (hm : m = r ^ L)
  |     (hs : s = s' * r ^ k) (hoff : s % q ≠ 0) :
  |     (((List.range N).filter (fun u => u % p != 0)).filter
  |       (fun u => !(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)).length
  |       = cnt (r ^ (L - k)) 1 N := by
  |   calc (((List.range N).filter (fun u => u % p != 0)).filter
  |           (fun u => !(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)).length
  |         = ((List.range N).filter (fun u => (u % p != 0)
  |             && (!(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0))).length := by
  |           rw [filter_filter']
  |       _ = ((List.range N).filter (fun u => u % r ^ (L - k) == 1)).length := by
  |           rw [filter_congr' (fun u => (u % p != 0)
  |                 && (!(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0))
  |               (fun u => u % r ^ (L - k) == 1) (List.range N)
  |               (fun u _ => point r e E L k s' p q N m s u hr he hs' hk hEL hp hq hN hm hs hoff)]
  |       _ = cnt (r ^ (L - k)) 1 N := rfl
  | 
  | theorem grid_factor_N (r E k : Nat) (hk : k < E) : r ^ (E + E) = r ^ (E + E - k) * r ^ k := by
  |   rw [← B329.pow_add', sub_add_cancel' (Nat.le_trans (Nat.le_of_lt hk) (Nat.le_add_right E E))]
  | 
  | theorem grid_factor_q (r E k : Nat) (hk : k < E) : r ^ (E + E) = r ^ (E - k) * (r ^ E * r ^ k) := by
  |   rw [B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm (r ^ (E - k)) (r ^ E), ← B329.mul_assoc', ← B329.pow_add' r (E - k) k,
  |       sub_add_cancel' (Nat.le_of_lt hk), ← B329.pow_add']
  | 
  | end N418

  | #print axioms N418.grid_factor_N

  | namespace N418
  | open SinglePrimeFactor
  | 
  | theorem filter_len_zero {α : Type} (P : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, (∀ x, x ∈ l → P x = false) → (l.filter P).length = 0 := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro _; rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     intro h
  |     rw [filter_cons_f P x l (h x (List.Mem.head _))]
  |     exact ih (fun y hy => h y (List.Mem.tail _ hy))
  | 
  | theorem sumf_mul {α : Type} (c : Nat) (f : α → Nat) : ∀ l : List α, sumf (fun x => c * f x) l = c * sumf f l := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rw [sumf_nil, sumf_nil, Nat.mul_zero]
  |   | cons x l ih => rw [sumf_cons, sumf_cons, ih, Nat.mul_add]
  | 
  | /-- THE GENERAL CLAUSE, AS `SinglePrimeFactor.lean` NAMES IT: for every base `p` with
  |     `singlePrimeFactor p = true` and every level `n`, `ballQ p n * sumAN p n = sumAQ p n`. -/
  | theorem smear_general (p n : Nat) (h : singlePrimeFactor p = true) :
  |     B329.ballQ p n * B329.sumAN p n = B329.sumAQ p n := by
  |   obtain ⟨r, e, hr, he, hp⟩ := spf_power p h
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   have hq : B329.ballQ p n = r ^ (e * n) := by
  |     show p ^ n = r ^ (e * n)
  |     rw [hp, pow_mul']
  |   have hNr : B329.gridN p n = r ^ (e * n + e * n) := by
  |     show p ^ (2 * n) = r ^ (e * n + e * n)
  |     rw [hp, pow_mul', Nat.two_mul, Nat.mul_add]
  |   have hNqq : B329.gridN p n = B329.ballQ p n * B329.ballQ p n := by rw [hNr, hq, B329.pow_add']
  |   rw [sumAN_swap, sumAQ_swap, ← sumf_mul]
  |   apply sumf_congr
  |   intro s _
  |   dsimp only
  |   cases hb : (s % B329.ballQ p n == 0) with
  |   | true =>
  |     have cN0 : ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.gridN p n == 0)).length = 0 :=
  |       filter_len_zero _ _ (fun u _ => by rw [hb]; all_goals rfl)
  |     have cQ0 : ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.ballQ p n == 0)).length = 0 :=
  |       filter_len_zero _ _ (fun u _ => by rw [hb]; all_goals rfl)
  |     rw [cN0, cQ0, Nat.mul_zero]
  |   | false =>
  |     have hoff : s % B329.ballQ p n ≠ 0 := by
  |       intro hz
  |       rw [hz] at hb
  |       exact Bool.noConfusion hb
  |     obtain ⟨k, s', hs, hns', hk⟩ := offball_val r e n s hr2 (by rw [← hq]; exact hoff)
  |     have hs' : s' % r ≠ 0 := mod_of_notDiv hr0 hns'
  |     have hkNN : k < e * n + e * n := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le hk (Nat.le_add_right _ _)
  |     have hD1 : 1 < r ^ (e * n + e * n - k) :=
  |       Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2) (pow_ge r _ hr0 (one_le_sub hkNN))
  |     have hD2 : 1 < r ^ (e * n - k) :=
  |       Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2) (pow_ge r _ hr0 (one_le_sub hk))
  |     have cN : ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.gridN p n == 0)).length
  |         = r ^ k :=
  |       (per_s_count r e (e * n) (e * n + e * n) k s' p (B329.ballQ p n) (B329.gridN p n) (B329.gridN p n) s
  |         hr he hs' hk (Nat.le_add_right _ _) hp hq hNqq hNr hs hoff).trans
  |       (count_is (r ^ (e * n + e * n - k)) (r ^ k) (B329.gridN p n) hD1
  |         (by rw [hNr]; exact grid_factor_N r (e * n) k hk))
  |     have cQ : ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.ballQ p n == 0)).length
  |         = r ^ (e * n) * r ^ k :=
  |       (per_s_count r e (e * n) (e * n) k s' p (B329.ballQ p n) (B329.gridN p n) (B329.ballQ p n) s
  |         hr he hs' hk (Nat.le_refl _) hp hq hNqq hq hs hoff).trans
  |       (count_is (r ^ (e * n - k)) (r ^ (e * n) * r ^ k) (B329.gridN p n) hD2
  |         (by rw [hNr]; exact grid_factor_q r (e * n) k hk))
  |     rw [cN, cQ, hq]
  | 
  | /-- THE SEVEN DECIDED CELLS ARE INSTANCES: every cell of `B329.cells` has a single prime factor,
  |     so the general clause covers each of them. -/

  | -- b418 (N) probe 16, THE LAST: probe 15's four slips repaired -- the general clause and the seven cells.

  | theorem sub_add_cancel' : ∀ {x y : Nat}, y ≤ x → x - y + y = x := by
  |   intro x y
  |   induction y generalizing x with
  |   | zero => intro _; rfl
  |   | succ y ih =>
  |     intro h
  |     cases x with
  |     | zero => exact absurd h (Nat.not_succ_le_zero y)
  |     | succ x => rw [Nat.succ_sub_succ, Nat.add_succ, ih (Nat.le_of_succ_le_succ h)]
  | 
  | theorem go_spec (y : Nat) (hy : 0 < y) : ∀ fuel x (hf : x < fuel),
  |     ∃ q, x = y * q + Nat.modCore.go y hy fuel x hf ∧ Nat.modCore.go y hy fuel x hf < y := by
  |   intro fuel
  |   induction fuel with
  |   | zero => intro x hf; exact absurd hf (Nat.not_lt_zero x)
  |   | succ f ih =>
  |     intro x hf
  |     rw [Nat.modCore.go.eq_1]
  |     by_cases h : y ≤ x
  |     · rw [dif_pos h]
  |       have hf' : x - y < f :=
  |         Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.sub_lt (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le hy h) hy) (Nat.le_of_lt_succ hf)
  |       obtain ⟨q, hq, hr⟩ := ih (x - y) hf'
  |       refine ⟨q + 1, ?_, hr⟩
  |       rw [Nat.mul_succ, Nat.add_right_comm, ← hq, sub_add_cancel' h]
  |     · rw [dif_neg h]
  |       exact ⟨0, by rw [Nat.mul_zero, Nat.zero_add], Nat.lt_of_not_le h⟩
  | 
  | theorem mod_spec (a m : Nat) (hm : 0 < m) : ∃ q, a = m * q + a % m ∧ a % m < m := by
  |   cases a with
  |   | zero => exact ⟨0, by rw [Nat.zero_mod, Nat.mul_zero], by rw [Nat.zero_mod]; exact hm⟩
  |   | succ n =>
  |     have e : (n + 1) % m = if m ≤ n + 1 then Nat.modCore (n + 1) m else n + 1 := Nat.mod.eq_2 m n
  |     rw [e]
  |     by_cases h : m ≤ n + 1
  |     · rw [if_pos h]
  |       have e2 : Nat.modCore (n + 1) m = Nat.modCore.go m hm (n + 1 + 1) (n + 1) (Nat.lt_succ_self _) := by
  |         rw [Nat.modCore.eq_def, dif_pos hm]
  |       rw [e2]
  |       exact go_spec m hm _ _ _
  |     · rw [if_neg h]
  |       exact ⟨0, by rw [Nat.mul_zero, Nat.zero_add], Nat.lt_of_not_le h⟩
  | 
  | theorem mod_zero_witness (a m : Nat) (hm : 0 < m) (h : a % m = 0) : ∃ c, a = m * c := by
  |   obtain ⟨q, hq, _⟩ := mod_spec a m hm
  |   rw [h, Nat.add_zero] at hq
  |   exact ⟨q, hq⟩
  |

  | -- (A) THE REMAINDER IS UNIQUE.
  | theorem div_rem_unique (m : Nat) : ∀ q q' r r', m * q + r = m * q' + r' → r < m → r' < m → r = r' := by
  |   intro q
  |   induction q with
  |   | zero =>
  |     intro q' r r' h hr hr'
  |     cases q' with
  |     | zero => rw [Nat.mul_zero, Nat.zero_add, Nat.zero_add] at h; exact h
  |     | succ k =>
  |       exfalso
  |       rw [Nat.mul_zero, Nat.zero_add, Nat.mul_succ] at h
  |       have : m ≤ r := by rw [h]; exact Nat.le_trans (Nat.le_add_left m (m * k)) (Nat.le_add_right _ r')
  |       exact Nat.lt_irrefl _ (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le hr this)
  |   | succ k ih =>
  |     intro q' r r' h hr hr'
  |     cases q' with
  |     | zero =>
  |       exfalso
  |       rw [Nat.mul_zero, Nat.zero_add, Nat.mul_succ] at h
  |       have : m ≤ r' := by rw [← h]; exact Nat.le_trans (Nat.le_add_left m (m * k)) (Nat.le_add_right _ r)
  |       exact Nat.lt_irrefl _ (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le hr' this)
  |     | succ k' =>
  |       rw [Nat.mul_succ, Nat.mul_succ, Nat.add_right_comm, Nat.add_right_comm (m * k')] at h
  |       exact ih k' r r' (B329.add_right_cancel' h) hr hr'
  | 
  | theorem mod_unique (a m q r : Nat) (hm : 0 < m) (hr : r < m) (h : a = m * q + r) : a % m = r := by
  |   obtain ⟨q', hq', hr'⟩ := mod_spec a m hm
  |   exact (div_rem_unique m q' q (a % m) r (by rw [← hq', ← h]) hr' hr)
  | 
  | theorem mul_mod_self' (m c : Nat) (hm : 0 < m) : (m * c) % m = 0 :=
  |   mod_unique (m * c) m c 0 hm hm (by rw [Nat.add_zero])
  | 
  | theorem add_mul_mod' (a m c : Nat) (hm : 0 < m) : (a + m * c) % m = a % m := by
  |   obtain ⟨q, hq, hr⟩ := mod_spec a m hm
  |   apply mod_unique _ m (q + c) _ hm hr
  |   rw [Nat.mul_add, Nat.add_right_comm, ← hq]

  | -- (B) THE LIST LEMMAS.
  | theorem filter_cons_t {α : Type} (p : α → Bool) (a : α) (l : List α) (h : p a = true) :
  |     (a :: l).filter p = a :: l.filter p := by rw [List.filter, h]
  | 
  | theorem filter_cons_f {α : Type} (p : α → Bool) (a : α) (l : List α) (h : p a = false) :
  |     (a :: l).filter p = l.filter p := by rw [List.filter, h]
  | 
  | theorem mem_filter_pred {α : Type} (p : α → Bool) (x : α) : ∀ l : List α, x ∈ l.filter p → p x = true := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro hm; cases hm
  |   | cons a l ih =>
  |     intro hm
  |     cases hp : p a with
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_t p a l hp] at hm
  |       cases hm with
  |       | head => exact hp
  |       | tail _ h' => exact ih h'
  |     | false =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_f p a l hp] at hm
  |       exact ih hm
  | 
  | theorem length_one {α : Type} : ∀ l : List α, l.length = 1 → ∃ a, l = [a]
  |   | [], h => Nat.noConfusion h
  |   | [a], _ => ⟨a, rfl⟩
  |   | _ :: _ :: t, h => Nat.noConfusion (Nat.succ.inj h)

  | theorem beq_true_eq {a b : Nat} (h : (a == b) = true) : a = b := by
  |   first
  |   | exact Nat.eq_of_beq_eq_true h
  |   | exact of_decide_eq_true h
  | 
  | theorem and_true_split {a b : Bool} (h : (a && b) = true) : a = true ∧ b = true := by
  |   cases a <;> cases b <;> first | exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩ | exact absurd h (by decide)
  | 
  | theorem loop_mem_to (x : Nat) : ∀ n (acc : List Nat), x ∈ List.range.loop n acc → x < n ∨ x ∈ acc := by
  |   intro n
  |   induction n with
  |   | zero => intro acc h; exact Or.inr h
  |   | succ n ih =>
  |     intro acc h
  |     rw [List.range.loop.eq_2] at h
  |     cases ih (n :: acc) h with
  |     | inl hl => exact Or.inl (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le hl (Nat.le_succ n))
  |     | inr hm =>
  |       cases hm with
  |       | head => exact Or.inl (Nat.lt_succ_self _)
  |       | tail _ h' => exact Or.inr h'
  | 
  | theorem loop_mem_of (x : Nat) : ∀ n (acc : List Nat), (x < n ∨ x ∈ acc) → x ∈ List.range.loop n acc := by
  |   intro n
  |   induction n with
  |   | zero =>
  |     intro acc h
  |     cases h with
  |     | inl hl => exact absurd hl (Nat.not_lt_zero x)
  |     | inr hm => exact hm
  |   | succ n ih =>
  |     intro acc h
  |     rw [List.range.loop.eq_2]
  |     apply ih
  |     cases h with
  |     | inl hl =>
  |       cases Nat.lt_or_ge x n with
  |       | inl hx => exact Or.inl hx
  |       | inr hx =>
  |         have e : x = n := Nat.le_antisymm (Nat.le_of_lt_succ hl) hx
  |         rw [e]; exact Or.inr (List.Mem.head _)
  |     | inr hm => exact Or.inr (List.Mem.tail _ hm)
  | 
  | theorem mem_range_of_lt {x n : Nat} (h : x < n) : x ∈ List.range n := by
  |   rw [List.range.eq_1]; exact loop_mem_of x n [] (Or.inl h)
  | 
  | theorem lt_of_mem_range {x n : Nat} (h : x ∈ List.range n) : x < n := by
  |   rw [List.range.eq_1] at h
  |   cases loop_mem_to x n [] h with
  |   | inl hl => exact hl
  |   | inr hm => cases hm
  | 
  | theorem mem_filter_of {α : Type} (p : α → Bool) (x : α) : ∀ l : List α, x ∈ l → p x = true → x ∈ l.filter p := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro hm; cases hm
  |   | cons a l ih =>
  |     intro hm hp
  |     cases hpa : p a with
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_t p a l hpa]
  |       cases hm with
  |       | head => exact List.Mem.head _
  |       | tail _ h' => exact List.Mem.tail _ (ih h' hp)
  |     | false =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_f p a l hpa]
  |       cases hm with
  |       | head => rw [hp] at hpa; exact absurd hpa (by decide)
  |       | tail _ h' => exact ih h' hp
  | 
  | theorem all_mem {α : Type} (f : α → Bool) (x : α) : ∀ l : List α, l.all f = true → x ∈ l → f x = true := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro _ hm; cases hm
  |   | cons a l ih =>
  |     intro h hm
  |     rw [List.all.eq_2] at h
  |     obtain ⟨ha, hl⟩ := and_true_split h
  |     cases hm with
  |     | head => exact ha
  |     | tail _ h' => exact ih hl h'
  | 
  | theorem isPrime_two_le {d : Nat} (h : isPrime d = true) : 2 ≤ d := by
  |   cases d with
  |   | zero => exact absurd h (by decide)
  |   | succ d =>
  |     cases d with
  |     | zero => exact absurd h (by decide)
  |     | succ d => exact Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.zero_le d))
  | 
  | theorem isPrime_nodiv {d : Nat} (h : isPrime d = true) (k : Nat) (hk : 2 ≤ k) (hkd : k < d) : d % k ≠ 0 := by
  |   unfold isPrime at h
  |   have hall : (List.range d).all (fun k => k == 0 || k == 1 || d % k != 0) = true := (and_true_split h).2
  |   have hk' := all_mem _ k _ hall (mem_range_of_lt hkd)
  |   intro hz
  |   cases k with
  |   | zero => exact absurd hk (by decide)
  |   | succ k =>
  |     cases k with
  |     | zero => exact absurd hk (by decide)
  |     | succ k =>
  |       have hz' : d % (k + 1 + 1) = 0 := hz
  |       rw [hz'] at hk'
  |       exact Bool.noConfusion hk'
  | 
  | theorem spf_prime (p : Nat) (h : singlePrimeFactor p = true) :
  |     ∃ r, isPrime r = true ∧ p % r = 0 ∧ ∀ d, isPrime d = true → p % d = 0 → d ≤ p → d = r := by
  |   have hl : (primeDivisors p).length = 1 := by unfold singlePrimeFactor at h; exact beq_true_eq h
  |   obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := length_one _ hl
  |   have hmem : r ∈ primeDivisors p := by rw [hr]; exact List.Mem.head _
  |   have hpr := mem_filter_pred _ r _ hmem
  |   obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := and_true_split hpr
  |   refine ⟨r, h1, beq_true_eq h2, ?_⟩
  |   intro d hd hpd hdp
  |   have hin : d ∈ primeDivisors p := by
  |     unfold primeDivisors
  |     apply mem_filter_of _ d _ (mem_range_of_lt (Nat.lt_succ_of_le hdp))
  |     show (isPrime d && p % d == 0) = true
  |     rw [hd, hpd]
  |     decide
  |   rw [hr] at hin
  |   cases hin with
  |   | head => rfl
  |   | tail _ h' => cases h'
  | 
  | 
  | theorem all_false_exists {α : Type} (f : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, l.all f = false → ∃ x, x ∈ l ∧ f x = false := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro h; exact Bool.noConfusion h
  |   | cons a l ih =>
  |     intro h
  |     rw [List.all.eq_2] at h
  |     cases hfa : f a with
  |     | false => exact ⟨a, List.Mem.head _, hfa⟩
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [hfa] at h
  |       obtain ⟨x, hx, hfx⟩ := ih h
  |       exact ⟨x, List.Mem.tail _ hx, hfx⟩
  | 
  | theorem mod_self' (m : Nat) (hm : 0 < m) : m % m = 0 := by
  |   have e := mul_mod_self' m 1 hm
  |   rw [Nat.mul_one] at e
  |   exact e
  | 
  | theorem mod_trans {a b d : Nat} (hb : 0 < b) (hd : 0 < d) (hab : a % b = 0) (hbd : b % d = 0) : a % d = 0 := by
  |   obtain ⟨c1, h1⟩ := mod_zero_witness a b hb hab
  |   obtain ⟨c2, h2⟩ := mod_zero_witness b d hd hbd
  |   rw [h1, h2, ← B329.mul_assoc']
  |   exact mul_mod_self' d _ hd
  | 
  | theorem le_of_mod_zero {a d : Nat} (ha : 0 < a) (hd : 0 < d) (h : a % d = 0) : d ≤ a := by
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness a d hd h
  |   cases c with
  |   | zero => rw [Nat.mul_zero] at hc; rw [hc] at ha; exact absurd ha (Nat.lt_irrefl 0)
  |   | succ c => rw [hc, Nat.mul_succ]; exact Nat.le_add_left d (d * c)
  | 
  | theorem exists_prime_div : ∀ m, 2 ≤ m → ∃ d, isPrime d = true ∧ m % d = 0 ∧ d ≤ m := by
  |   intro m
  |   induction m using Nat.strongRecOn with
  |   | _ m ih =>
  |     intro hm
  |     have hm0 : 0 < m := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hm
  |     cases hp : isPrime m with
  |     | true => exact ⟨m, hp, mod_self' m hm0, Nat.le_refl m⟩
  |     | false =>
  |       cases m with
  |       | zero => exact absurd hm (by decide)
  |       | succ m1 =>
  |         cases m1 with
  |         | zero => exact absurd hm (by decide)
  |         | succ k =>
  |           have hall : (List.range (k + 1 + 1)).all
  |               (fun j => j == 0 || j == 1 || (k + 1 + 1) % j != 0) = false := hp
  |           obtain ⟨j, hj, hfj⟩ := all_false_exists _ _ hall
  |           have hjm : j < k + 1 + 1 := lt_of_mem_range hj
  |           cases j with
  |           | zero => exact Bool.noConfusion hfj
  |           | succ j1 =>
  |             cases j1 with
  |             | zero => exact Bool.noConfusion hfj
  |             | succ i =>
  |               have hdiv : (k + 1 + 1) % (i + 1 + 1) = 0 := by
  |                 cases hb : ((k + 1 + 1) % (i + 1 + 1) == 0) with
  |                 | true => exact beq_true_eq hb
  |                 | false =>
  |                   have hf2 : ((k + 1 + 1) % (i + 1 + 1) != 0) = false := hfj
  |                   rw [bne, hb] at hf2
  |                   exact Bool.noConfusion hf2
  |               have hi2 : 2 ≤ i + 1 + 1 := Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.zero_le i))
  |               obtain ⟨d, hd, hid, hdi⟩ := ih (i + 1 + 1) hjm hi2
  |               have hd0 : 0 < d := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) (isPrime_two_le hd)
  |               exact ⟨d, hd, mod_trans (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hi2) hd0 hdiv hid,
  |                      Nat.le_trans hdi (Nat.le_of_lt hjm)⟩
  | 
  | theorem spf_power (p : Nat) (h : singlePrimeFactor p = true) :
  |     ∃ r e, isPrime r = true ∧ 1 ≤ e ∧ p = r ^ e := by
  |   obtain ⟨r, hr, hpr, huniq⟩ := spf_prime p h
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   cases p with
  |   | zero => exact absurd h (by decide)
  |   | succ p' =>
  |     obtain ⟨j, u, hju, hu⟩ := B329.valuation_exists r hr2 (p' + 1) (Nat.succ_pos p')
  |     have hu0 : 0 < u := by
  |       cases u with
  |       | zero => rw [Nat.zero_mul] at hju; exact Nat.noConfusion hju
  |       | succ u' => exact Nat.succ_pos u'
  |     have hu1 : u = 1 := by
  |       cases Nat.lt_or_ge u 2 with
  |       | inl hlt => exact Nat.le_antisymm (Nat.le_of_lt_succ hlt) hu0
  |       | inr hge =>
  |         exfalso
  |         obtain ⟨d, hd, hud, _⟩ := exists_prime_div u hge
  |         have hd0 : 0 < d := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) (isPrime_two_le hd)
  |         obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness u d hd0 hud
  |         have hpd : (p' + 1) % d = 0 := by
  |           rw [hju, hc, ← B329.mul_assoc']
  |           exact mul_mod_self' d _ hd0
  |         have hdp : d ≤ p' + 1 := le_of_mod_zero (Nat.succ_pos p') hd0 hpd
  |         have e := huniq d hd hpd hdp
  |         rw [e] at hc
  |         exact hu c hc
  |     refine ⟨r, j, hr, ?_, ?_⟩
  |     · cases j with
  |       | zero =>
  |         exfalso
  |         rw [Nat.pow_zero, Nat.mul_one, hu1] at hju
  |         rw [hju] at hpr
  |         have e1 : 1 % r = 1 := mod_unique 1 r 0 1 hr0 hr2 (by rw [Nat.mul_zero, Nat.zero_add])
  |         rw [e1] at hpr
  |         exact Nat.noConfusion hpr
  |       | succ j' => exact Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.zero_le j')
  |     · rw [hju, hu1, Nat.one_mul]
  | 
  | 
  | theorem mul_pos' {a b : Nat} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) : 0 < a * b := by
  |   cases a with
  |   | zero => exact absurd ha (Nat.lt_irrefl 0)
  |   | succ a =>
  |     cases b with
  |     | zero => exact absurd hb (Nat.lt_irrefl 0)
  |     | succ b => rw [Nat.mul_succ]; exact Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.succ_pos a) (Nat.le_add_left _ _)
  | 
  | theorem pow_pos' (r : Nat) (hr : 0 < r) : ∀ j, 0 < r ^ j := by
  |   intro j
  |   induction j with
  |   | zero => rw [Nat.pow_zero]; exact Nat.succ_pos 0
  |   | succ j ih => rw [Nat.pow_succ]; exact mul_pos' ih hr
  | 
  | theorem euclid_lt (r b : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) : ∀ a, 0 < a → a < r → (a * b) % r = 0 → b % r = 0 := by
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   intro a
  |   induction a using Nat.strongRecOn with
  |   | _ a ih =>
  |     intro ha0 har hab
  |     cases Nat.lt_or_ge a 2 with
  |     | inl h1 =>
  |       have e : a = 1 := Nat.le_antisymm (Nat.le_of_lt_succ h1) ha0
  |       rw [e, Nat.one_mul] at hab
  |       exact hab
  |     | inr h2 =>
  |       have ha0' : 0 < a := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) h2
  |       obtain ⟨q, hq, ht⟩ := mod_spec r a ha0'
  |       have hnz : r % a ≠ 0 := isPrime_nodiv hr a h2 har
  |       generalize hg : r % a = t at hq ht hnz
  |       have ht0 : 0 < t := by
  |         cases t with
  |         | zero => exact absurd rfl hnz
  |         | succ z => exact Nat.succ_pos z
  |       apply ih t ht ht0 (Nat.lt_trans ht har)
  |       obtain ⟨c1, hc1⟩ := mod_zero_witness (a * b) r hr0 hab
  |       have e : t * b + r * (c1 * q) = r * b := by
  |         calc t * b + r * (c1 * q) = t * b + r * c1 * q := by rw [B329.mul_assoc']
  |           _ = t * b + a * b * q := by rw [← hc1]
  |           _ = t * b + a * q * b := by rw [← B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm b q, B329.mul_assoc']
  |           _ = (a * q + t) * b := by
  |               rw [Nat.mul_comm (a * q + t) b, Nat.mul_add, Nat.mul_comm b (a * q), Nat.mul_comm b t, Nat.add_comm]
  |           _ = r * b := by rw [← hq]
  |       obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := B329.multiple_of_add_multiple (c1 * q) b e
  |       rw [hd]
  |       exact mul_mod_self' r d hr0
  | 
  | theorem euclid (r a b : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (hab : (a * b) % r = 0) (ha : a % r ≠ 0) : b % r = 0 := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) (isPrime_two_le hr)
  |   obtain ⟨q, hq, hlt⟩ := mod_spec a r hr0
  |   generalize hg : a % r = a' at hq hlt ha
  |   have ha0 : 0 < a' := by
  |     cases a' with
  |     | zero => exact absurd rfl ha
  |     | succ z => exact Nat.succ_pos z
  |   apply euclid_lt r b hr a' ha0 hlt
  |   obtain ⟨c1, hc1⟩ := mod_zero_witness (a * b) r hr0 hab
  |   have e : a' * b + r * (q * b) = r * c1 := by
  |     calc a' * b + r * (q * b) = a' * b + r * q * b := by rw [B329.mul_assoc']
  |       _ = (r * q + a') * b := by
  |           rw [Nat.mul_comm (r * q + a') b, Nat.mul_add, Nat.mul_comm b (r * q), Nat.mul_comm b a', Nat.add_comm]
  |       _ = a * b := by rw [← hq]
  |       _ = r * c1 := hc1
  |   obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := B329.multiple_of_add_multiple (q * b) c1 e
  |   rw [hd]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' r d hr0
  | 
  | theorem euclid_pow (r s : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (hs : s % r ≠ 0) :
  |     ∀ j x, (x * s) % (r ^ j) = 0 → x % (r ^ j) = 0 := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) (isPrime_two_le hr)
  |   intro j
  |   induction j with
  |   | zero =>
  |     intro x _
  |     rw [Nat.pow_zero]
  |     exact mod_unique x 1 x 0 (by decide) (by decide) (by rw [Nat.one_mul, Nat.add_zero])
  |   | succ j ih =>
  |     intro x h
  |     have hrj0 : 0 < r ^ (j + 1) := pow_pos' r hr0 (j + 1)
  |     obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness (x * s) (r ^ (j + 1)) hrj0 h
  |     have hr_xs : (s * x) % r = 0 := by
  |       rw [Nat.mul_comm s x, hc, Nat.pow_succ, Nat.mul_comm (r ^ j) r, ← B329.mul_assoc']
  |       exact mul_mod_self' r _ hr0
  |     have hrx : x % r = 0 := euclid r s x hr hr_xs hs
  |     obtain ⟨x1, hx1⟩ := mod_zero_witness x r hr0 hrx
  |     have e : r * (x1 * s) = r * (r ^ j * c) := by
  |       rw [B329.mul_assoc', ← hx1, hc, Nat.pow_succ, Nat.mul_comm (r ^ j) r, ← B329.mul_assoc']
  |     have e2 : x1 * s = r ^ j * c := Nat.eq_of_mul_eq_mul_left hr0 e
  |     have h1 : (x1 * s) % (r ^ j) = 0 := by rw [e2]; exact mul_mod_self' _ _ (pow_pos' r hr0 j)
  |     obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := mod_zero_witness x1 (r ^ j) (pow_pos' r hr0 j) (ih x1 h1)
  |     rw [hx1, hd, B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm r (r ^ j), ← Nat.pow_succ]
  |     exact mul_mod_self' _ _ hrj0
  | 
  | 
  | def ind (b : Bool) : Nat := match b with | true => 1 | false => 0
  | def sumf {α : Type} (f : α → Nat) (l : List α) : Nat := l.foldl (fun acc x => acc + f x) 0
  | 
  | theorem foldl_shift {α : Type} (f : α → Nat) : ∀ (l : List α) (a : Nat),
  |     l.foldl (fun acc x => acc + f x) a = a + l.foldl (fun acc x => acc + f x) 0 := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro a; rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     intro a
  |     rw [List.foldl_cons, List.foldl_cons, ih (a + f x), ih (0 + f x), Nat.zero_add, Nat.add_assoc]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_nil {α : Type} (f : α → Nat) : sumf f [] = 0 := rfl
  | 
  | theorem sumf_cons {α : Type} (f : α → Nat) (x : α) (l : List α) : sumf f (x :: l) = f x + sumf f l := by
  |   unfold sumf
  |   rw [List.foldl_cons, foldl_shift f l (0 + f x), Nat.zero_add]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_congr {α : Type} (f g : α → Nat) : ∀ l : List α, (∀ x, x ∈ l → f x = g x) → sumf f l = sumf g l := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro _; rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     intro h
  |     rw [sumf_cons, sumf_cons, h x (List.Mem.head _), ih (fun y hy => h y (List.Mem.tail _ hy))]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_zero {α : Type} : ∀ l : List α, sumf (fun _ => 0) l = 0 := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l ih => rw [sumf_cons, ih]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_add {α : Type} (f g : α → Nat) : ∀ l : List α, sumf (fun x => f x + g x) l = sumf f l + sumf g l := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     rw [sumf_cons, sumf_cons, sumf_cons, ih, Nat.add_add_add_comm]
  | 
  | theorem length_filter_ind {α : Type} (P : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, (l.filter P).length = sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     rw [sumf_cons]
  |     cases hp : P x with
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_t P x l hp, List.length_cons, ih]
  |       show sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l + 1 = 1 + sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l
  |       rw [Nat.add_comm]
  |     | false =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_f P x l hp, ih]
  |       show sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l = 0 + sumf (fun x => ind (P x)) l
  |       rw [Nat.zero_add]
  | 
  | theorem sumf_swap {α β : Type} (g : α → β → Nat) (S : List β) : ∀ U : List α,
  |     sumf (fun u => sumf (fun s => g u s) S) U = sumf (fun s => sumf (fun u => g u s) U) S := by
  |   intro U
  |   induction U with
  |   | nil => rw [sumf_nil]; exact (sumf_zero S).symm
  |   | cons u U ih =>
  |     rw [sumf_cons, ih,
  |         sumf_congr (fun s => sumf (fun u' => g u' s) (u :: U)) (fun s => g u s + sumf (fun u' => g u' s) U) S
  |           (fun s _ => sumf_cons _ u U),
  |         sumf_add]
  | 
  | theorem count_swap {α β : Type} (P : α → β → Bool) (U : List α) (S : List β) :
  |     sumf (fun u => (S.filter (P u)).length) U = sumf (fun s => (U.filter (fun u => P u s)).length) S := by
  |   rw [sumf_congr _ (fun u => sumf (fun s => ind (P u s)) S) U (fun u _ => length_filter_ind (P u) S),
  |       sumf_congr _ (fun s => sumf (fun u => ind (P u s)) U) S (fun s _ => length_filter_ind (fun u => P u s) U)]
  |   exact sumf_swap (fun u s => ind (P u s)) S U
  | 
  | theorem sumA_swap (p n m : Nat) :
  |     (B329.units p n).foldl (fun acc u => acc + B329.offBallFixed p n u m) 0 =
  |     sumf (fun s => ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)).length)
  |       (List.range (B329.gridN p n)) :=
  |   count_swap (fun u s => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)
  |     (B329.units p n) (List.range (B329.gridN p n))
  | 
  | theorem sumAN_swap (p n : Nat) : B329.sumAN p n =
  |     sumf (fun s => ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.gridN p n == 0)).length)
  |       (List.range (B329.gridN p n)) := sumA_swap p n (B329.gridN p n)
  | 
  | theorem sumAQ_swap (p n : Nat) : B329.sumAQ p n =
  |     sumf (fun s => ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.ballQ p n == 0)).length)
  |       (List.range (B329.gridN p n)) := sumA_swap p n (B329.ballQ p n)
  | 
  | 
  | theorem append_assoc' {α : Type} : ∀ (a b c : List α), (a ++ b) ++ c = a ++ (b ++ c) := by
  |   intro a b c
  |   induction a with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x a ih => show x :: ((a ++ b) ++ c) = x :: (a ++ (b ++ c)); rw [ih]
  | 
  | theorem length_append' {α : Type} : ∀ (l1 l2 : List α), (l1 ++ l2).length = l1.length + l2.length := by
  |   intro l1 l2
  |   induction l1 with
  |   | nil => show l2.length = 0 + l2.length; rw [Nat.zero_add]
  |   | cons x l1 ih =>
  |     show (x :: (l1 ++ l2)).length = (x :: l1).length + l2.length
  |     rw [List.length_cons, List.length_cons, ih, Nat.add_right_comm]
  | 
  | theorem filter_append' {α : Type} (P : α → Bool) : ∀ (l1 l2 : List α), (l1 ++ l2).filter P = l1.filter P ++ l2.filter P := by
  |   intro l1 l2
  |   induction l1 with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l1 ih =>
  |     show (x :: (l1 ++ l2)).filter P = (x :: l1).filter P ++ l2.filter P
  |     cases hp : P x with
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_t P x _ hp, filter_cons_t P x _ hp, ih]
  |       all_goals rfl
  |     | false => rw [filter_cons_f P x _ hp, filter_cons_f P x _ hp, ih]
  | 
  | theorem loop_append : ∀ (n : Nat) (acc : List Nat), List.range.loop n acc = List.range.loop n [] ++ acc := by
  |   intro n
  |   induction n with
  |   | zero => intro acc; rfl
  |   | succ n ih =>
  |     intro acc
  |     rw [List.range.loop.eq_2, List.range.loop.eq_2, ih (n :: acc), ih [n], append_assoc']
  |     all_goals rfl
  | 
  | theorem range_succ' (n : Nat) : List.range (n + 1) = List.range n ++ [n] := by
  |   rw [List.range.eq_1, List.range.eq_1, List.range.loop.eq_2, loop_append n [n]]
  | 
  | def cnt (d t N : Nat) : Nat := ((List.range N).filter (fun u => u % d == t)).length
  | 
  | theorem cnt_succ (d t N : Nat) : cnt d t (N + 1) = cnt d t N + ind (N % d == t) := by
  |   unfold cnt
  |   rw [range_succ', filter_append', length_append']
  |   cases h : (N % d == t) with
  |   | true => rw [filter_cons_t _ N [] h]; all_goals rfl
  |   | false => rw [filter_cons_f _ N [] h]; all_goals rfl
  | 
  | theorem ne_beq_false {a b : Nat} (h : a ≠ b) : (a == b) = false := by
  |   cases hb : (a == b) with
  |   | true => exact absurd (beq_true_eq hb) h
  |   | false => rfl
  | 
  | theorem ind_step (t i : Nat) : ind (decide (t < i)) + ind (i == t) = ind (decide (t < i + 1)) := by
  |   cases Nat.lt_or_ge t i with
  |   | inl h1 =>
  |     have hne : i ≠ t := fun e => Nat.lt_irrefl t (by rw [e] at h1; exact h1)
  |     rw [decide_eq_true h1, ne_beq_false hne, decide_eq_true (Nat.lt_succ_of_le (Nat.le_of_lt h1))]
  |     all_goals rfl
  |   | inr h2 =>
  |     cases Nat.lt_or_ge i t with
  |     | inl h3 =>
  |       have hne : i ≠ t := fun e => Nat.lt_irrefl t (by rw [e] at h3; exact h3)
  |       have hn1 : ¬ t < i + 1 := fun h => Nat.lt_irrefl t (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le h (Nat.succ_le_of_lt h3))
  |       have hn0 : ¬ t < i := fun h => Nat.lt_irrefl t (Nat.lt_trans h h3)
  |       rw [decide_eq_false hn0, ne_beq_false hne, decide_eq_false hn1]
  |       all_goals rfl
  |     | inr h4 =>
  |       have e : i = t := Nat.le_antisymm h2 h4
  |       have hn0 : ¬ t < i := fun h => Nat.lt_irrefl t (by rw [e] at h; exact h)
  |       rw [decide_eq_false hn0, e, decide_eq_true (Nat.lt_succ_self t)]
  |       have hb : (t == t) = true := by first | exact Nat.beq_refl t | exact decide_eq_true rfl
  |       rw [hb]
  |       all_goals rfl
  | 
  | theorem blk (d t c : Nat) (hd : 0 < d) (h : cnt d t (d * c) = c) :
  |     ∀ i, i ≤ d → cnt d t (d * c + i) = c + ind (decide (t < i)) := by
  |   intro i
  |   induction i with
  |   | zero => intro _; rw [Nat.add_zero, h]; all_goals rfl
  |   | succ i ih =>
  |     intro hi
  |     have hi' : i < d := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.lt_succ_self i) hi
  |     rw [← Nat.add_assoc, cnt_succ, ih (Nat.le_of_lt hi'),
  |         mod_unique (d * c + i) d c i hd hi' rfl, Nat.add_assoc, ind_step]
  | 
  | theorem ap_count (d t : Nat) (hd : 0 < d) (ht : t < d) : ∀ c, cnt d t (d * c) = c := by
  |   intro c
  |   induction c with
  |   | zero => rw [Nat.mul_zero]; rfl
  |   | succ c ih =>
  |     have e := blk d t c hd ih d (Nat.le_refl d)
  |     rw [decide_eq_true ht] at e
  |     rw [Nat.mul_succ, e]
  |     all_goals rfl
  | 
  | 
  | theorem filter_congr' {α : Type} (P Q : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, (∀ x, x ∈ l → P x = Q x) → l.filter P = l.filter Q := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro _; rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     intro h
  |     have hx := h x (List.Mem.head _)
  |     have ht := ih (fun y hy => h y (List.Mem.tail _ hy))
  |     cases hp : P x with
  |     | true => rw [filter_cons_t P x l hp, filter_cons_t Q x l (by rw [← hx, hp]), ht]
  |     | false => rw [filter_cons_f P x l hp, filter_cons_f Q x l (by rw [← hx, hp]), ht]
  | 
  | theorem filter_filter' {α : Type} (P Q : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, (l.filter P).filter Q = l.filter (fun x => P x && Q x) := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     cases hp : P x with
  |     | true =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_t P x l hp]
  |       cases hq : Q x with
  |       | true =>
  |         rw [filter_cons_t Q x _ hq,
  |             filter_cons_t (fun x => P x && Q x) x l (by show (P x && Q x) = true; rw [hp, hq]; all_goals rfl), ih]
  |       | false =>
  |         rw [filter_cons_f Q x _ hq,
  |             filter_cons_f (fun x => P x && Q x) x l (by show (P x && Q x) = false; rw [hp, hq]; all_goals rfl), ih]
  |     | false =>
  |       rw [filter_cons_f P x l hp,
  |           filter_cons_f (fun x => P x && Q x) x l (by show (P x && Q x) = false; rw [hp]; all_goals rfl), ih]
  | 
  | theorem pow_mul' (r e : Nat) : ∀ n, (r ^ e) ^ n = r ^ (e * n) := by
  |   intro n
  |   induction n with
  |   | zero => rw [Nat.pow_zero, Nat.mul_zero, Nat.pow_zero]
  |   | succ n ih => rw [Nat.pow_succ, ih, Nat.mul_succ, B329.pow_add']
  | 
  | theorem mod_mod_of_mul (a q k : Nat) (hq : 0 < q) (hk : 0 < k) : (a % (q * k)) % q = a % q := by
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc, _⟩ := mod_spec a (q * k) (mul_pos' hq hk)
  |   have e : a = a % (q * k) + q * (k * c) := by rw [B329.mul_assoc', Nat.add_comm]; exact hc
  |   calc (a % (q * k)) % q = (a % (q * k) + q * (k * c)) % q := (add_mul_mod' _ q _ hq).symm
  |     _ = a % q := by rw [← e]
  | 
  | theorem pow_cancel (r x k L : Nat) (hr : 0 < r) (hkL : k ≤ L) (h : (x * r ^ k) % (r ^ L) = 0) :
  |     x % (r ^ (L - k)) = 0 := by
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness _ _ (pow_pos' r hr L) h
  |   have e : r ^ L = r ^ (L - k) * r ^ k := by rw [← B329.pow_add', sub_add_cancel' hkL]
  |   have e2 : x * r ^ k = r ^ (L - k) * c * r ^ k := by
  |     rw [hc, e, ← B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm (r ^ k) c, B329.mul_assoc']
  |   have e3 : x = r ^ (L - k) * c := Nat.eq_of_mul_eq_mul_right (pow_pos' r hr k) e2
  |   rw [e3]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' _ _ (pow_pos' r hr _)
  | 
  | theorem offball_val (r e n s : Nat) (hr2 : 2 ≤ r) (hs : s % (r ^ (e * n)) ≠ 0) :
  |     ∃ k s', s = s' * r ^ k ∧ B329.NotDiv r s' ∧ k < e * n := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   have hs0 : 0 < s := by
  |     cases s with
  |     | zero => exact absurd (Nat.zero_mod _) hs
  |     | succ s => exact Nat.succ_pos s
  |   obtain ⟨j, u, hju, hu⟩ := B329.valuation_exists r hr2 s hs0
  |   refine ⟨j, u, hju, hu, ?_⟩
  |   apply Nat.lt_of_not_le
  |   intro hle
  |   apply hs
  |   have e1 : r ^ j = r ^ (j - e * n) * r ^ (e * n) := by rw [← B329.pow_add', sub_add_cancel' hle]
  |   rw [hju, e1, B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm _ (r ^ (e * n))]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' _ _ (pow_pos' r hr0 _)
  | 
  | theorem bne_true_ne {a b : Nat} (h : (a != b) = true) : a ≠ b := by
  |   intro e
  |   rw [e] at h
  |   have hb : (b == b) = true := by first | exact Nat.beq_refl b | exact decide_eq_true rfl
  |   rw [bne, hb] at h
  |   exact Bool.noConfusion h
  | 
  | theorem ne_bne_true {a b : Nat} (h : a ≠ b) : (a != b) = true := by
  |   rw [bne, ne_beq_false h]; all_goals rfl
  | 
  | theorem notDiv_of_mod {r u : Nat} (hr : 0 < r) (h : u % r ≠ 0) : B329.NotDiv r u := by
  |   intro c hc
  |   apply h
  |   rw [hc]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' r c hr
  | 
  | theorem mod_of_notDiv {r u : Nat} (hr : 0 < r) (h : B329.NotDiv r u) : u % r ≠ 0 := by
  |   intro hz
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness u r hr hz
  |   exact h c hc
  | 
  | 
  | theorem one_le_sub {k L : Nat} (h : k < L) : 1 ≤ L - k := by
  |   cases hz : L - k with
  |   | zero =>
  |     exfalso
  |     have e := sub_add_cancel' (Nat.le_of_lt h)
  |     rw [hz, Nat.zero_add] at e
  |     rw [e] at h
  |     exact Nat.lt_irrefl L h
  |   | succ z => exact Nat.succ_le_succ (Nat.zero_le z)
  | 
  | theorem pow_pred (r j : Nat) (hj : 1 ≤ j) : r ^ j = r ^ (j - 1) * r :=
  |   (congrArg (fun x => r ^ x) (sub_add_cancel' hj)).symm.trans Nat.pow_succ
  | 
  | theorem pow_ge (r j : Nat) (hr : 0 < r) (hj : 1 ≤ j) : r ≤ r ^ j := by
  |   have e : r ^ j = r ^ (j - 1) * r := pow_pred r j hj
  |   rw [e]
  |   have h1 : 1 ≤ r ^ (j - 1) := pow_pos' r hr (j - 1)
  |   calc r = 1 * r := (Nat.one_mul r).symm
  |     _ ≤ r ^ (j - 1) * r := Nat.mul_le_mul_right r h1
  | 
  | theorem pow_mod_self (r j : Nat) (hr : 0 < r) (hj : 1 ≤ j) : (r ^ j) % r = 0 := by
  |   have e : r ^ j = r * r ^ (j - 1) := (pow_pred r j hj).trans (Nat.mul_comm _ _)
  |   rw [e]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' r _ hr
  | 
  | theorem mod_r_one (r u j : Nat) (hr2 : 2 ≤ r) (hj : 1 ≤ j) (h : u % r ^ j = 1) : u % r = 1 := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc, _⟩ := mod_spec u (r ^ j) (pow_pos' r hr0 j)
  |   rw [h] at hc
  |   have e : r ^ j = r * r ^ (j - 1) := (pow_pred r j hj).trans (Nat.mul_comm _ _)
  |   rw [e, ← B329.mul_assoc'] at hc
  |   exact mod_unique u r (r ^ (j - 1) * c) 1 hr0 (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2) hc
  | 
  | theorem mod_one_not_dvd (r u j : Nat) (hr2 : 2 ≤ r) (hj : 1 ≤ j) (h : u % r = 1) : u % r ^ j ≠ 0 := by
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   intro hz
  |   have e := mod_trans (pow_pos' r hr0 j) hr0 hz (pow_mod_self r j hr0 hj)
  |   rw [h] at e
  |   exact Nat.noConfusion e
  | 
  | theorem cong_of (r s' k L u : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (hs' : s' % r ≠ 0) (hkL : k < L) (hu : 0 < u)
  |     (h : ((u - 1) * (s' * r ^ k)) % (r ^ L) = 0) : u % (r ^ (L - k)) = 1 := by
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   rw [B329.mul_assoc'] at h
  |   have h2 := pow_cancel r ((u - 1) * s') k L hr0 (Nat.le_of_lt hkL) h
  |   have h3 := euclid_pow r s' hr hs' (L - k) (u - 1) h2
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mod_zero_witness (u - 1) (r ^ (L - k)) (pow_pos' r hr0 _) h3
  |   have hu1 : 1 ≤ u := hu
  |   have e : u = r ^ (L - k) * c + 1 := by rw [← hc, sub_add_cancel' hu1]
  |   exact mod_unique u (r ^ (L - k)) c 1 (pow_pos' r hr0 _)
  |     (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2) (pow_ge r (L - k) hr0 (one_le_sub hkL))) e
  | 
  | theorem cong_to (r s' k L u : Nat) (hr0 : 0 < r) (hkL : k ≤ L) (h : u % (r ^ (L - k)) = 1) :
  |     ((u - 1) * (s' * r ^ k)) % (r ^ L) = 0 := by
  |   obtain ⟨c, hc, _⟩ := mod_spec u (r ^ (L - k)) (pow_pos' r hr0 _)
  |   rw [h] at hc
  |   have e1 : u - 1 = r ^ (L - k) * c := by rw [hc]; all_goals rfl
  |   have e2 : (u - 1) * (s' * r ^ k) = r ^ L * (c * s') := by
  |     rw [e1]
  |     calc r ^ (L - k) * c * (s' * r ^ k) = r ^ (L - k) * (c * (s' * r ^ k)) :=
  |           (B329.mul_assoc' (r ^ (L - k)) c (s' * r ^ k)).symm
  |       _ = r ^ (L - k) * (c * s' * r ^ k) := congrArg (fun x => r ^ (L - k) * x) (B329.mul_assoc' c s' (r ^ k))
  |       _ = r ^ (L - k) * (r ^ k * (c * s')) := congrArg (fun x => r ^ (L - k) * x) (Nat.mul_comm (c * s') (r ^ k))
  |       _ = r ^ (L - k) * r ^ k * (c * s') := B329.mul_assoc' (r ^ (L - k)) (r ^ k) (c * s')
  |       _ = r ^ L * (c * s') := by rw [← B329.pow_add', sub_add_cancel' hkL]
  |   rw [e2]
  |   exact mul_mod_self' _ _ (pow_pos' r hr0 L)
  | 
  | theorem offball_of (r s' k E u : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (hs' : s' % r ≠ 0) (hk : k < E) (hu : u % r = 1) :
  |     (u * (s' * r ^ k)) % (r ^ E) ≠ 0 := by
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   intro hz
  |   rw [B329.mul_assoc'] at hz
  |   have h2 := pow_cancel r (u * s') k E hr0 (Nat.le_of_lt hk) hz
  |   have h3 := euclid_pow r s' hr hs' (E - k) u h2
  |   exact mod_one_not_dvd r u (E - k) hr2 (one_le_sub hk) hu h3
  | 
  | theorem point (r e E L k s' p q N m s u : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (he : 1 ≤ e) (hs' : s' % r ≠ 0)
  |     (hk : k < E) (hEL : E ≤ L) (hp : p = r ^ e) (hq : q = r ^ E) (hN : N = q * q) (hm : m = r ^ L)
  |     (hs : s = s' * r ^ k) (hoff : s % q ≠ 0) :
  |     ((u % p != 0) && (!(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0))
  |       = (u % r ^ (L - k) == 1) := by
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   have hkL : k < L := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le hk hEL
  |   cases hb : (u % r ^ (L - k) == 1) with
  |   | true =>
  |     have h1 : u % r ^ (L - k) = 1 := beq_true_eq hb
  |     have hur : u % r = 1 := mod_r_one r u (L - k) hr2 (one_le_sub hkL) h1
  |     have c1 : (u % p != 0) = true := ne_bne_true (by rw [hp]; exact mod_one_not_dvd r u e hr2 he hur)
  |     have c2 : (!(s % q == 0)) = true := by rw [ne_beq_false hoff]; all_goals rfl
  |     have hq0 : 0 < q := by rw [hq]; exact pow_pos' r hr0 E
  |     have hus : (u * s) % q ≠ 0 := by rw [hs, hq]; exact offball_of r s' k E u hr hs' hk hur
  |     have c3 : (!((u * s) % N % q == 0)) = true := by
  |       rw [hN, mod_mod_of_mul (u * s) q q hq0 hq0, ne_beq_false hus]; all_goals rfl
  |     have c4 : (((u - 1) * s) % m == 0) = true := by
  |       rw [hs, hm, cong_to r s' k L u hr0 (Nat.le_of_lt hkL) h1]; all_goals rfl
  |     rw [c1, c2, c3, c4]
  |     all_goals rfl
  |   | false =>
  |     cases hL : ((u % p != 0) && (!(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)) with
  |     | false => rfl
  |     | true =>
  |       exfalso
  |       obtain ⟨a1, a234⟩ := and_true_split hL
  |       obtain ⟨_, a4⟩ := and_true_split a234
  |       have hu0 : 0 < u := by
  |         cases u with
  |         | zero => exact absurd (Nat.zero_mod p) (bne_true_ne a1)
  |         | succ u' => exact Nat.succ_pos u'
  |       have h4 : ((u - 1) * s) % m = 0 := beq_true_eq a4
  |       rw [hs, hm] at h4
  |       have h5 := cong_of r s' k L u hr hs' hkL hu0 h4
  |       rw [h5] at hb
  |       exact Bool.noConfusion hb
  | 
  | theorem count_is (D c N : Nat) (hD : 1 < D) (h : N = D * c) : cnt D 1 N = c := by
  |   rw [h]
  |   exact ap_count D 1 (Nat.lt_trans (by decide) hD) hD c
  | 
  | theorem per_s_count (r e E L k s' p q N m s : Nat) (hr : isPrime r = true) (he : 1 ≤ e) (hs' : s' % r ≠ 0)
  |     (hk : k < E) (hEL : E ≤ L) (hp : p = r ^ e) (hq : q = r ^ E) (hN : N = q * q) (hm : m = r ^ L)
  |     (hs : s = s' * r ^ k) (hoff : s % q ≠ 0) :
  |     (((List.range N).filter (fun u => u % p != 0)).filter
  |       (fun u => !(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)).length
  |       = cnt (r ^ (L - k)) 1 N := by
  |   calc (((List.range N).filter (fun u => u % p != 0)).filter
  |           (fun u => !(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0)).length
  |         = ((List.range N).filter (fun u => (u % p != 0)
  |             && (!(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0))).length := by
  |           rw [filter_filter']
  |       _ = ((List.range N).filter (fun u => u % r ^ (L - k) == 1)).length := by
  |           rw [filter_congr' (fun u => (u % p != 0)
  |                 && (!(s % q == 0) && !((u * s) % N % q == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % m == 0))
  |               (fun u => u % r ^ (L - k) == 1) (List.range N)
  |               (fun u _ => point r e E L k s' p q N m s u hr he hs' hk hEL hp hq hN hm hs hoff)]
  |       _ = cnt (r ^ (L - k)) 1 N := rfl
  | 
  | theorem grid_factor_N (r E k : Nat) (hk : k < E) : r ^ (E + E) = r ^ (E + E - k) * r ^ k := by
  |   rw [← B329.pow_add', sub_add_cancel' (Nat.le_trans (Nat.le_of_lt hk) (Nat.le_add_right E E))]
  | 
  | theorem grid_factor_q (r E k : Nat) (hk : k < E) : r ^ (E + E) = r ^ (E - k) * (r ^ E * r ^ k) := by
  |   rw [B329.mul_assoc', Nat.mul_comm (r ^ (E - k)) (r ^ E), ← B329.mul_assoc', ← B329.pow_add' r (E - k) k,
  |       sub_add_cancel' (Nat.le_of_lt hk), ← B329.pow_add']
  | 
  | end N418

  | namespace N418
  | open SinglePrimeFactor
  | 
  | theorem filter_len_zero {α : Type} (P : α → Bool) : ∀ l : List α, (∀ x, x ∈ l → P x = false) → (l.filter P).length = 0 := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => intro _; rfl
  |   | cons x l ih =>
  |     intro h
  |     rw [filter_cons_f P x l (h x (List.Mem.head _))]
  |     exact ih (fun y hy => h y (List.Mem.tail _ hy))
  | 
  | theorem sumf_mul {α : Type} (c : Nat) (f : α → Nat) : ∀ l : List α, sumf (fun x => c * f x) l = c * sumf f l := by
  |   intro l
  |   induction l with
  |   | nil => rw [sumf_nil, sumf_nil, Nat.mul_zero]
  |   | cons x l ih => rw [sumf_cons, sumf_cons, ih, Nat.mul_add]
  | 
  | /-- THE GENERAL CLAUSE, AS `SinglePrimeFactor.lean` NAMES IT: for every base `p` with
  |     `singlePrimeFactor p = true` and every level `n`, `ballQ p n * sumAN p n = sumAQ p n`. -/
  | theorem smear_general (p n : Nat) (h : singlePrimeFactor p = true) :
  |     B329.ballQ p n * B329.sumAN p n = B329.sumAQ p n := by
  |   obtain ⟨r, e, hr, he, hp⟩ := spf_power p h
  |   have hr2 := isPrime_two_le hr
  |   have hr0 : 0 < r := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2
  |   have hq : B329.ballQ p n = r ^ (e * n) := by
  |     show p ^ n = r ^ (e * n)
  |     rw [hp, pow_mul']
  |   have hNr : B329.gridN p n = r ^ (e * n + e * n) := by
  |     show p ^ (2 * n) = r ^ (e * n + e * n)
  |     rw [hp, pow_mul', Nat.two_mul, Nat.mul_add]
  |   have hNqq : B329.gridN p n = B329.ballQ p n * B329.ballQ p n := by rw [hNr, hq, B329.pow_add']
  |   rw [sumAN_swap, sumAQ_swap, ← sumf_mul]
  |   apply sumf_congr
  |   intro s _
  |   dsimp only
  |   by_cases hz : s % B329.ballQ p n = 0
  |   · have hb : (s % B329.ballQ p n == 0) = true := by rw [hz]; all_goals rfl
  |     have cN0 : ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.gridN p n == 0)).length = 0 :=
  |       filter_len_zero _ _ (fun u _ => by rw [hb]; all_goals rfl)
  |     have cQ0 : ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.ballQ p n == 0)).length = 0 :=
  |       filter_len_zero _ _ (fun u _ => by rw [hb]; all_goals rfl)
  |     rw [cN0, cQ0, Nat.mul_zero]
  |   · have hoff : s % B329.ballQ p n ≠ 0 := hz
  |     obtain ⟨k, s', hs, hns', hk⟩ := offball_val r e n s hr2 (by rw [← hq]; exact hoff)
  |     have hs' : s' % r ≠ 0 := mod_of_notDiv hr0 hns'
  |     have hkNN : k < e * n + e * n := Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le hk (Nat.le_add_right _ _)
  |     have hD1 : 1 < r ^ (e * n + e * n - k) :=
  |       Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2) (pow_ge r _ hr0 (one_le_sub hkNN))
  |     have hD2 : 1 < r ^ (e * n - k) :=
  |       Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (Nat.lt_of_lt_of_le (by decide) hr2) (pow_ge r _ hr0 (one_le_sub hk))
  |     have cN : ((B329.units p n).filter (fun u => !(s % B329.ballQ p n == 0)
  |         && !((u * s) % B329.gridN p n % B329.ballQ p n == 0) && ((u - 1) * s) % B329.gridN p n == 0)).length
  |         = r ^ k :=
  |       (per_s_count r e (e * n) (e * n + e * n) k s' p (B329.ballQ p n) (B329.gridN p n) (B329.gridN p n) s
  |         hr he hs' hk (Nat.le_add_right _ _) hp hq hNqq hNr hs hoff).trans
  |       (count_is (r ^ (e * n + e * n - k)) (r ^ k) (B329.gridN p n) hD1
